The highest temperature was 84&deg;, on the 26th: and the lowest 46&deg;, on the 16th. S.E. winds prevailed. Rain fell on 19 days. 192 CUSTOMS GAZETTE. [APRIL-JUNE, (Received 13th July 1891.) CUSTOM HOUSE, KOWLOON, 9TH July 1891. SIR, I.&mdash;a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 381, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Kowloon and the neighbouring stations in Native vessels for the second quarter of the year 1891, April-June. b. The Custom House (^L H 11) was opened on the 2nd April 1887, and the collection of Provincial Likin and Ching-fei Tax on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Provincial Tariff received from the Viceroy of the Two Kwang, began on that day. The collection of Tariff Import Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (Hk.Tts. 30 and Hk.Th 80 per picul) commenced on the 14th April 1887, and the collection of Native Duties on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Native Custom House Tariff received from the Hoppo, on the ist July 1887. The stations at present controlled by the Kowloon Customs are known as the Capsuimoon P^), Changchow (J| ^jj), Fotochow ({$ H $H), and Kowloon (jl g&sect;) stations. c. The word &quot; Inwards &quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot; Outwards,&quot; from China. II. 212 CUSTOMS GAZETTE. [JAN.&mdash;MAR., (Received 24th April 1891.) CUSTOM HOUSE, LUNGCHOW, 2nd April 1891. SIR, &bull; I.&mdash;In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circulars Nos. 21 of 1869, 14 of 1872, and 6 of 1875, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Lungchow for the first quarter of the year 1891. II. REPORTS TO THE CUSTOMS (i.e., Arrivals and Departures). January. February. March. Total. Inwards 41 19 21 81 Outwards 13 12 11 36 Total 54 31 32 117 Same Quarter, 1890 21 20 30 71 During the quarter 56 chartered junks, 224 tons, loaded with French Government stores, passed Lungchow on their way to Thatke and Caobang. 1891.] MENGTSZ. 215 (Received 15 thMay 1891.) CUSTOM HOUSE, MENGTSZ, 17th April 1891. SIR, I.&mdash;a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circulars Nos. 21 of 1869, 14 of 1872, and 6 of 1875,1 now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Mengtsz, Manhao, and Mapai-kuan for the first quarter of the year 1891. b. The head office (fH &sect; ||) and the branch office at Manhao (&sect;&sect;| were opened on the 24th August 1889; the branch office at Mapai-kuan m &amp;amp; id on the 19th September, three weeks later.
